=== exercise mesh-uvsphere
title: 4. UV sphere
continues: mesh-render
--- prompt
Write `uvSphere(r, seg, rings)`:

- Vertices `v = j * (seg + 1) + i` for rings `j = 0..rings` and segments `i = 0..seg` (the last column duplicates the first: the UV seam). With `θ = π j / rings`, `φ = 2π i / seg`: normal `(sin θ cos φ, cos θ, sin θ sin φ)`, position `r ×` normal, uv `(i/seg, j/rings)`.
- For each quad `a = j*(seg+1)+i`, `b = a+1`, `c = a+seg+1`, `d = c+1` (j, then i): the triangles `(a, b, c)` and `(b, d, c)`, except that the top ring (`j = 0`) skips `(a, b, c)` and the bottom ring (`j = rings − 1`) skips `(b, d, c)`: those are degenerate at the poles.

So the triangle count is `2 · seg · (rings − 1)`; allocate exactly that. Check that the triangles face outward.
--- stubs
function uvSphere(r, seg, rings) {
  return createMesh(0, 0);
}
--- solution
// ---- From earlier chapters: seeded PRNG and 3D Perlin noise + fBm (p2-noise) ----
function mulberry32(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
// Improved Perlin noise (Perlin 2002) with a seeded permutation table. Returns roughly [-1, 1].
function makePerlin3(seed) {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed), p = new Uint8Array(512), perm = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) perm[i] = i;
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--) { const j = (rng() * (i + 1)) | 0, t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) p[i] = perm[i & 255];
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + t * (b - a);
  const grad = (h, x, y, z) => {
    h &= 15;
    const u = h < 8 ? x : y, v = h < 4 ? y : (h === 12 || h === 14 ? x : z);
    return ((h & 1) ? -u : u) + ((h & 2) ? -v : v);
  };
  return function (x, y, z) {
    const X = Math.floor(x), Y = Math.floor(y), Z = Math.floor(z);
    x -= X; y -= Y; z -= Z;
    const xi = X & 255, yi = Y & 255, zi = Z & 255;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y), w = fade(z);
    const A = p[xi] + yi, AA = p[A] + zi, AB = p[A + 1] + zi, B = p[xi + 1] + yi, BA = p[B] + zi, BB = p[B + 1] + zi;
    return lerp(
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA], x, y, z), grad(p[BA], x - 1, y, z), u), lerp(grad(p[AB], x, y - 1, z), grad(p[BB], x - 1, y - 1, z), u), v),
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA + 1], x, y, z - 1), grad(p[BA + 1], x - 1, y, z - 1), u), lerp(grad(p[AB + 1], x, y - 1, z - 1), grad(p[BB + 1], x - 1, y - 1, z - 1), u), v),
      w);
  };
}
function fbm3(noise, x, y, z, octaves = 5, lacunarity = 2, gain = 0.5) {
  let sum = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1;
  for (let o = 0; o < octaves; o++) {
    sum += amp * noise(x * freq, y * freq, z * freq);
    freq *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
  }
  return sum;
}

// Indexed triangle mesh: every array allocated once at its final size.
function createMesh(nv, nt) {
  return {
    positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2),
    indices: new Uint32Array(nt * 3),
  };
}

function gridMesh(w, d, nx, nz) {
  const m = createMesh((nx + 1) * (nz + 1), nx * nz * 2);
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= nx; i++, v++) {
      P[v * 3] = -w / 2 + w * i / nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = 0; P[v * 3 + 2] = -d / 2 + d * j / nz;
      N[v * 3 + 1] = 1;
      U[v * 2] = i / nx; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / nz;
    }
  }
  for (let j = 0, k = 0; j < nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      const a = j * (nx + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + nx + 1, e = c + 1;
      I[k++] = a; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = b;
      I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = e;
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// Smooth normals: sum the unnormalized face normals (length = 2 x area) into each corner, then normalize.
function computeNormals(mesh) {
  const P = mesh.positions, I = mesh.indices, N = mesh.normals;
  N.fill(0);
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    N[a] += nx; N[a + 1] += ny; N[a + 2] += nz;
    N[b] += nx; N[b + 1] += ny; N[b + 2] += nz;
    N[c] += nx; N[c + 1] += ny; N[c + 2] += nz;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N.length; i += 3) {
    const l = Math.hypot(N[i], N[i + 1], N[i + 2]);
    if (l > 0) { N[i] /= l; N[i + 1] /= l; N[i + 2] /= l; }
  }
  return mesh;
}

// Flat shading: every triangle gets its own three vertices carrying the face normal.
function flatShade(mesh) {
  const I = mesh.indices, P = mesh.positions, U = mesh.uvs, nt = I.length / 3;
  const m = createMesh(nt * 3, nt);
  const P2 = m.positions, N2 = m.normals, U2 = m.uvs;
  for (let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const s = I[t * 3 + k], d = t * 3 + k;
      P2[d * 3] = P[s * 3]; P2[d * 3 + 1] = P[s * 3 + 1]; P2[d * 3 + 2] = P[s * 3 + 2];
      U2[d * 2] = U[s * 2]; U2[d * 2 + 1] = U[s * 2 + 1];
      m.indices[d] = d;
    }
    const a = t * 9;
    const ux = P2[a + 3] - P2[a], uy = P2[a + 4] - P2[a + 1], uz = P2[a + 5] - P2[a + 2];
    const vx = P2[a + 6] - P2[a], vy = P2[a + 7] - P2[a + 1], vz = P2[a + 8] - P2[a + 2];
    let nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1;
    nx /= l; ny /= l; nz /= l;
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k += 3) { N2[a + k] = nx; N2[a + k + 1] = ny; N2[a + k + 2] = nz; }
  }
  return m;
}

// Column-major 4x4 matrices (the layout WebGPU uses), depth range 0..1.
function perspective(fovy, aspect, near, far) {
  const f = 1 / Math.tan(fovy / 2), m = new Float32Array(16);
  m[0] = f / aspect; m[5] = f; m[10] = far / (near - far); m[11] = -1; m[14] = near * far / (near - far);
  return m;
}
function lookAt(eye, target, up) {
  let zx = eye[0] - target[0], zy = eye[1] - target[1], zz = eye[2] - target[2];
  let l = Math.hypot(zx, zy, zz); zx /= l; zy /= l; zz /= l;
  let xx = up[1] * zz - up[2] * zy, xy = up[2] * zx - up[0] * zz, xz = up[0] * zy - up[1] * zx;
  l = Math.hypot(xx, xy, xz); xx /= l; xy /= l; xz /= l;
  const yx = zy * xz - zz * xy, yy = zz * xx - zx * xz, yz = zx * xy - zy * xx;
  return new Float32Array([xx, yx, zx, 0, xy, yy, zy, 0, xz, yz, zz, 0,
    -(xx * eye[0] + xy * eye[1] + xz * eye[2]), -(yx * eye[0] + yy * eye[1] + yz * eye[2]), -(zx * eye[0] + zy * eye[1] + zz * eye[2]), 1]);
}
function mul4(a, b) {   // a * b
  const m = new Float32Array(16);
  for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++)
    m[c * 4 + r] = a[r] * b[c * 4] + a[4 + r] * b[c * 4 + 1] + a[8 + r] * b[c * 4 + 2] + a[12 + r] * b[c * 4 + 3];
  return m;
}
// A render target: RGBA8 color plus a float depth buffer.
function createTarget(width, height) {
  return { width, height, color: new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4), depth: new Float32Array(width * height) };
}
function clearTarget(t, r, g, b) {
  const c = t.color;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i += 4) { c[i] = r; c[i + 1] = g; c[i + 2] = b; c[i + 3] = 255; }
  t.depth.fill(Infinity);
}
function blit(ctx, t) {
  ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(t.color, t.width, t.height), 0, 0);
}

// Z-buffered, back-face-culled, Gouraud-shaded (per-vertex Lambert) rasterizer.
let scratch = new Float32Array(0);
function renderMesh(target, mesh, viewProj, opts = {}) {
  const color = opts.color ?? [200, 200, 200], ambient = opts.ambient ?? 0.2;
  let [lx, ly, lz] = opts.light ?? [0.4, 0.8, 0.45];
  const ll = Math.hypot(lx, ly, lz); lx /= ll; ly /= ll; lz /= ll;
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals, I = mesh.indices, nv = P.length / 3;
  const W = target.width, H = target.height, C = target.color, Z = target.depth, m = viewProj;
  if (scratch.length < nv * 4) scratch = new Float32Array(nv * 4);
  const S = scratch;   // per vertex: screen x, screen y, depth, intensity (w <= 0 marks it unusable)
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2];
    const cx = m[0] * x + m[4] * y + m[8] * z + m[12], cy = m[1] * x + m[5] * y + m[9] * z + m[13];
    const cz = m[2] * x + m[6] * y + m[10] * z + m[14], cw = m[3] * x + m[7] * y + m[11] * z + m[15];
    const d = N[v * 3] * lx + N[v * 3 + 1] * ly + N[v * 3 + 2] * lz;
    S[v * 4 + 3] = ambient + (1 - ambient) * (d > 0 ? d : 0);
    if (cw <= 1e-6) { S[v * 4 + 2] = NaN; continue; }
    S[v * 4] = (cx / cw * 0.5 + 0.5) * W;
    S[v * 4 + 1] = (0.5 - cy / cw * 0.5) * H;
    S[v * 4 + 2] = cz / cw;
  }
  const [cr, cg, cb] = color;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 4, b = I[t + 1] * 4, c = I[t + 2] * 4;
    const x0 = S[a], y0 = S[a + 1], z0 = S[a + 2], x1 = S[b], y1 = S[b + 1], z1 = S[b + 2], x2 = S[c], y2 = S[c + 1], z2 = S[c + 2];
    if (z0 !== z0 || z1 !== z1 || z2 !== z2) continue;              // behind the camera
    const area = (x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (x2 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
    if (area >= 0) continue;                                          // back face (y is down)
    let minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(x0, x1, x2))), maxX = Math.min(W - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(x0, x1, x2)));
    let minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(y0, y1, y2))), maxY = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(y0, y1, y2)));
    const i0 = S[a + 3], i1 = S[b + 3], i2 = S[c + 3], inv = 1 / area;
    for (let py = minY; py <= maxY; py++) {
      const sy = py + 0.5;
      for (let px = minX; px <= maxX; px++) {
        const sx = px + 0.5;
        const w0 = ((x2 - x1) * (sy - y1) - (y2 - y1) * (sx - x1)) * inv;
        const w1 = ((x0 - x2) * (sy - y2) - (y0 - y2) * (sx - x2)) * inv;
        const w2 = 1 - w0 - w1;
        if (w0 < 0 || w1 < 0 || w2 < 0) continue;
        const z = w0 * z0 + w1 * z1 + w2 * z2, p = py * W + px;
        if (z >= Z[p] || z < 0 || z > 1) continue;
        Z[p] = z;
        const k = w0 * i0 + w1 * i1 + w2 * i2, o = p * 4;
        C[o] = cr * k; C[o + 1] = cg * k; C[o + 2] = cb * k; C[o + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
  }
}

function uvSphere(r, seg, rings) {
  const m = createMesh((rings + 1) * (seg + 1), 2 * seg * (rings - 1));
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= rings; j++) {
    const th = Math.PI * j / rings, st = Math.sin(th), ct = Math.cos(th);
    for (let i = 0; i <= seg; i++, v++) {
      const ph = 2 * Math.PI * i / seg, nx = st * Math.cos(ph), nz = st * Math.sin(ph);
      N[v * 3] = nx; N[v * 3 + 1] = ct; N[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
      P[v * 3] = r * nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = r * ct; P[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
      U[v * 2] = i / seg; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / rings;
    }
  }
  let k = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < rings; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {
      const a = j * (seg + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + seg + 1, d = c + 1;
      if (j !== 0) { I[k++] = a; I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; }
      if (j !== rings - 1) { I[k++] = b; I[k++] = d; I[k++] = c; }
    }
  }
  return m;
}
--- hint
At the poles `sin θ = 0`, so a whole row of vertices sits at the same point; the triangles using two of them have zero area.
--- hint
Look at it: `const t = createTarget(canvas.width, canvas.height); clearTarget(t, 20, 24, 30); renderMesh(t, uvSphere(1, 32, 16), mul4(perspective(0.9, 1, 0.1, 100), lookAt([0, 1.5, 3], [0, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0])), { color: [230, 150, 90] }); blit(canvas.getContext('2d'), t);`
--- harness
{
  const s = uvSphere(2, 16, 8);
  eq(s.positions.length, 9 * 17 * 3, 'vertex count');
  eq(s.indices.length, 2 * 16 * 7 * 3, 'triangle count 2 seg (rings - 1)');
  const v = (j, i) => (j * 17 + i) * 3, R = a => Array.from(a, x => Math.round(x * 1e4) / 1e4 + 0);
  eq(R(s.positions.subarray(v(0, 5), v(0, 5) + 3)), [0, 2, 0], 'north pole');
  eq(R(s.positions.subarray(v(4, 0), v(4, 0) + 3)), [2, 0, 0], 'equator, phi = 0');
  eq(R(s.positions.subarray(v(4, 4), v(4, 4) + 3)), [0, 0, 2], 'equator, phi = 90 degrees');
  eq(R(s.positions.subarray(v(4, 16), v(4, 16) + 3)), [2, 0, 0], 'seam column duplicates the first');
  eq(R(s.normals.subarray(v(2, 3), v(2, 3) + 3)), R(Array.from(s.positions.subarray(v(2, 3), v(2, 3) + 3), x => x / 2)), 'normal = position / r');
  eq(R(s.uvs.subarray((3 * 17 + 4) * 2, (3 * 17 + 4) * 2 + 2)), [0.25, 0.375], 'uv');
  eq(Array.from(s.indices.subarray(0, 3)), [1, 18, 17], 'top ring starts with (b, d, c)');
  const P = s.positions, I = s.indices;
  let out = true, degenerate = false;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2], vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    if (Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) < 1e-6) degenerate = true;
    if (nx * (P[a] + P[b] + P[c]) + ny * (P[a + 1] + P[b + 1] + P[c + 1]) + nz * (P[a + 2] + P[b + 2] + P[c + 2]) <= 0) out = false;
  }
  check(out, 'every triangle faces outward');
  check(!degenerate, 'no degenerate triangles at the poles');
}
=== exercise mesh-icosphere
title: 5. Icosphere
continues: mesh-uvsphere
--- prompt
Write `icosphere(r, subdiv)`. The stubs add the icosahedron: `ICO_VERTS` (12 unnormalized points) and `ICO_FACES` (20 counterclockwise triangles).

- The result has exactly `10·4ⁿ + 2` vertices and `20·4ⁿ` triangles. Allocate the vertex storage once at the final size (build positions in a `Float64Array`, then write the `Float32Array`s at the end), and one index array per level.
- Each level replaces triangle `(a, b, c)` with `(a, ab, ca)`, `(b, bc, ab)`, `(c, ca, bc)`, `(ab, bc, ca)`, where `ab` is the midpoint vertex of edge a–b, pushed out to the sphere.
- Each edge's midpoint must be created **once** and shared by both triangles on that edge: keep a `Map` per level keyed by the single number `min(a,b) * nv + max(a,b)` (nv = final vertex count).
- Output: positions on the sphere of radius `r`, normals = unit position, and uvs `u = 0.5 + atan2(nz, nx) / 2π`, `v = acos(ny) / π` (the seam this mapping has is a known flaw; we accept it here).

The tests check the counts, that every vertex is on the sphere, outward faces, and that the mesh is closed (every edge used by exactly two triangles, once in each direction).
--- stubs
// The 12 vertices (unnormalized) and 20 outward-facing (counterclockwise) faces of an icosahedron.
const ICO_T = (1 + Math.sqrt(5)) / 2;
const ICO_VERTS = [-1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T,
  0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, ICO_T, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1];
const ICO_FACES = [0, 11, 5, 0, 5, 1, 0, 1, 7, 0, 7, 10, 0, 10, 11, 1, 5, 9, 5, 11, 4, 11, 10, 2, 10, 7, 6, 7, 1, 8,
  3, 9, 4, 3, 4, 2, 3, 2, 6, 3, 6, 8, 3, 8, 9, 4, 9, 5, 2, 4, 11, 6, 2, 10, 8, 6, 7, 9, 8, 1];

function icosphere(r, subdiv) {
  return createMesh(0, 0);
}
--- solution
// ---- From earlier chapters: seeded PRNG and 3D Perlin noise + fBm (p2-noise) ----
function mulberry32(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
// Improved Perlin noise (Perlin 2002) with a seeded permutation table. Returns roughly [-1, 1].
function makePerlin3(seed) {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed), p = new Uint8Array(512), perm = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) perm[i] = i;
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--) { const j = (rng() * (i + 1)) | 0, t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) p[i] = perm[i & 255];
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + t * (b - a);
  const grad = (h, x, y, z) => {
    h &= 15;
    const u = h < 8 ? x : y, v = h < 4 ? y : (h === 12 || h === 14 ? x : z);
    return ((h & 1) ? -u : u) + ((h & 2) ? -v : v);
  };
  return function (x, y, z) {
    const X = Math.floor(x), Y = Math.floor(y), Z = Math.floor(z);
    x -= X; y -= Y; z -= Z;
    const xi = X & 255, yi = Y & 255, zi = Z & 255;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y), w = fade(z);
    const A = p[xi] + yi, AA = p[A] + zi, AB = p[A + 1] + zi, B = p[xi + 1] + yi, BA = p[B] + zi, BB = p[B + 1] + zi;
    return lerp(
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA], x, y, z), grad(p[BA], x - 1, y, z), u), lerp(grad(p[AB], x, y - 1, z), grad(p[BB], x - 1, y - 1, z), u), v),
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA + 1], x, y, z - 1), grad(p[BA + 1], x - 1, y, z - 1), u), lerp(grad(p[AB + 1], x, y - 1, z - 1), grad(p[BB + 1], x - 1, y - 1, z - 1), u), v),
      w);
  };
}
function fbm3(noise, x, y, z, octaves = 5, lacunarity = 2, gain = 0.5) {
  let sum = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1;
  for (let o = 0; o < octaves; o++) {
    sum += amp * noise(x * freq, y * freq, z * freq);
    freq *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
  }
  return sum;
}

// Indexed triangle mesh: every array allocated once at its final size.
function createMesh(nv, nt) {
  return {
    positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2),
    indices: new Uint32Array(nt * 3),
  };
}

function gridMesh(w, d, nx, nz) {
  const m = createMesh((nx + 1) * (nz + 1), nx * nz * 2);
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= nx; i++, v++) {
      P[v * 3] = -w / 2 + w * i / nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = 0; P[v * 3 + 2] = -d / 2 + d * j / nz;
      N[v * 3 + 1] = 1;
      U[v * 2] = i / nx; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / nz;
    }
  }
  for (let j = 0, k = 0; j < nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      const a = j * (nx + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + nx + 1, e = c + 1;
      I[k++] = a; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = b;
      I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = e;
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// Smooth normals: sum the unnormalized face normals (length = 2 x area) into each corner, then normalize.
function computeNormals(mesh) {
  const P = mesh.positions, I = mesh.indices, N = mesh.normals;
  N.fill(0);
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    N[a] += nx; N[a + 1] += ny; N[a + 2] += nz;
    N[b] += nx; N[b + 1] += ny; N[b + 2] += nz;
    N[c] += nx; N[c + 1] += ny; N[c + 2] += nz;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N.length; i += 3) {
    const l = Math.hypot(N[i], N[i + 1], N[i + 2]);
    if (l > 0) { N[i] /= l; N[i + 1] /= l; N[i + 2] /= l; }
  }
  return mesh;
}

// Flat shading: every triangle gets its own three vertices carrying the face normal.
function flatShade(mesh) {
  const I = mesh.indices, P = mesh.positions, U = mesh.uvs, nt = I.length / 3;
  const m = createMesh(nt * 3, nt);
  const P2 = m.positions, N2 = m.normals, U2 = m.uvs;
  for (let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const s = I[t * 3 + k], d = t * 3 + k;
      P2[d * 3] = P[s * 3]; P2[d * 3 + 1] = P[s * 3 + 1]; P2[d * 3 + 2] = P[s * 3 + 2];
      U2[d * 2] = U[s * 2]; U2[d * 2 + 1] = U[s * 2 + 1];
      m.indices[d] = d;
    }
    const a = t * 9;
    const ux = P2[a + 3] - P2[a], uy = P2[a + 4] - P2[a + 1], uz = P2[a + 5] - P2[a + 2];
    const vx = P2[a + 6] - P2[a], vy = P2[a + 7] - P2[a + 1], vz = P2[a + 8] - P2[a + 2];
    let nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1;
    nx /= l; ny /= l; nz /= l;
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k += 3) { N2[a + k] = nx; N2[a + k + 1] = ny; N2[a + k + 2] = nz; }
  }
  return m;
}

// Column-major 4x4 matrices (the layout WebGPU uses), depth range 0..1.
function perspective(fovy, aspect, near, far) {
  const f = 1 / Math.tan(fovy / 2), m = new Float32Array(16);
  m[0] = f / aspect; m[5] = f; m[10] = far / (near - far); m[11] = -1; m[14] = near * far / (near - far);
  return m;
}
function lookAt(eye, target, up) {
  let zx = eye[0] - target[0], zy = eye[1] - target[1], zz = eye[2] - target[2];
  let l = Math.hypot(zx, zy, zz); zx /= l; zy /= l; zz /= l;
  let xx = up[1] * zz - up[2] * zy, xy = up[2] * zx - up[0] * zz, xz = up[0] * zy - up[1] * zx;
  l = Math.hypot(xx, xy, xz); xx /= l; xy /= l; xz /= l;
  const yx = zy * xz - zz * xy, yy = zz * xx - zx * xz, yz = zx * xy - zy * xx;
  return new Float32Array([xx, yx, zx, 0, xy, yy, zy, 0, xz, yz, zz, 0,
    -(xx * eye[0] + xy * eye[1] + xz * eye[2]), -(yx * eye[0] + yy * eye[1] + yz * eye[2]), -(zx * eye[0] + zy * eye[1] + zz * eye[2]), 1]);
}
function mul4(a, b) {   // a * b
  const m = new Float32Array(16);
  for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++)
    m[c * 4 + r] = a[r] * b[c * 4] + a[4 + r] * b[c * 4 + 1] + a[8 + r] * b[c * 4 + 2] + a[12 + r] * b[c * 4 + 3];
  return m;
}
// A render target: RGBA8 color plus a float depth buffer.
function createTarget(width, height) {
  return { width, height, color: new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4), depth: new Float32Array(width * height) };
}
function clearTarget(t, r, g, b) {
  const c = t.color;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i += 4) { c[i] = r; c[i + 1] = g; c[i + 2] = b; c[i + 3] = 255; }
  t.depth.fill(Infinity);
}
function blit(ctx, t) {
  ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(t.color, t.width, t.height), 0, 0);
}

// Z-buffered, back-face-culled, Gouraud-shaded (per-vertex Lambert) rasterizer.
let scratch = new Float32Array(0);
function renderMesh(target, mesh, viewProj, opts = {}) {
  const color = opts.color ?? [200, 200, 200], ambient = opts.ambient ?? 0.2;
  let [lx, ly, lz] = opts.light ?? [0.4, 0.8, 0.45];
  const ll = Math.hypot(lx, ly, lz); lx /= ll; ly /= ll; lz /= ll;
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals, I = mesh.indices, nv = P.length / 3;
  const W = target.width, H = target.height, C = target.color, Z = target.depth, m = viewProj;
  if (scratch.length < nv * 4) scratch = new Float32Array(nv * 4);
  const S = scratch;   // per vertex: screen x, screen y, depth, intensity (w <= 0 marks it unusable)
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2];
    const cx = m[0] * x + m[4] * y + m[8] * z + m[12], cy = m[1] * x + m[5] * y + m[9] * z + m[13];
    const cz = m[2] * x + m[6] * y + m[10] * z + m[14], cw = m[3] * x + m[7] * y + m[11] * z + m[15];
    const d = N[v * 3] * lx + N[v * 3 + 1] * ly + N[v * 3 + 2] * lz;
    S[v * 4 + 3] = ambient + (1 - ambient) * (d > 0 ? d : 0);
    if (cw <= 1e-6) { S[v * 4 + 2] = NaN; continue; }
    S[v * 4] = (cx / cw * 0.5 + 0.5) * W;
    S[v * 4 + 1] = (0.5 - cy / cw * 0.5) * H;
    S[v * 4 + 2] = cz / cw;
  }
  const [cr, cg, cb] = color;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 4, b = I[t + 1] * 4, c = I[t + 2] * 4;
    const x0 = S[a], y0 = S[a + 1], z0 = S[a + 2], x1 = S[b], y1 = S[b + 1], z1 = S[b + 2], x2 = S[c], y2 = S[c + 1], z2 = S[c + 2];
    if (z0 !== z0 || z1 !== z1 || z2 !== z2) continue;              // behind the camera
    const area = (x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (x2 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
    if (area >= 0) continue;                                          // back face (y is down)
    let minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(x0, x1, x2))), maxX = Math.min(W - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(x0, x1, x2)));
    let minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(y0, y1, y2))), maxY = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(y0, y1, y2)));
    const i0 = S[a + 3], i1 = S[b + 3], i2 = S[c + 3], inv = 1 / area;
    for (let py = minY; py <= maxY; py++) {
      const sy = py + 0.5;
      for (let px = minX; px <= maxX; px++) {
        const sx = px + 0.5;
        const w0 = ((x2 - x1) * (sy - y1) - (y2 - y1) * (sx - x1)) * inv;
        const w1 = ((x0 - x2) * (sy - y2) - (y0 - y2) * (sx - x2)) * inv;
        const w2 = 1 - w0 - w1;
        if (w0 < 0 || w1 < 0 || w2 < 0) continue;
        const z = w0 * z0 + w1 * z1 + w2 * z2, p = py * W + px;
        if (z >= Z[p] || z < 0 || z > 1) continue;
        Z[p] = z;
        const k = w0 * i0 + w1 * i1 + w2 * i2, o = p * 4;
        C[o] = cr * k; C[o + 1] = cg * k; C[o + 2] = cb * k; C[o + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
  }
}

function uvSphere(r, seg, rings) {
  const m = createMesh((rings + 1) * (seg + 1), 2 * seg * (rings - 1));
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= rings; j++) {
    const th = Math.PI * j / rings, st = Math.sin(th), ct = Math.cos(th);
    for (let i = 0; i <= seg; i++, v++) {
      const ph = 2 * Math.PI * i / seg, nx = st * Math.cos(ph), nz = st * Math.sin(ph);
      N[v * 3] = nx; N[v * 3 + 1] = ct; N[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
      P[v * 3] = r * nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = r * ct; P[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
      U[v * 2] = i / seg; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / rings;
    }
  }
  let k = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < rings; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {
      const a = j * (seg + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + seg + 1, d = c + 1;
      if (j !== 0) { I[k++] = a; I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; }
      if (j !== rings - 1) { I[k++] = b; I[k++] = d; I[k++] = c; }
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// The 12 vertices (unnormalized) and 20 outward-facing (counterclockwise) faces of an icosahedron.
const ICO_T = (1 + Math.sqrt(5)) / 2;
const ICO_VERTS = [-1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T,
  0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, ICO_T, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1];
const ICO_FACES = [0, 11, 5, 0, 5, 1, 0, 1, 7, 0, 7, 10, 0, 10, 11, 1, 5, 9, 5, 11, 4, 11, 10, 2, 10, 7, 6, 7, 1, 8,
  3, 9, 4, 3, 4, 2, 3, 2, 6, 3, 6, 8, 3, 8, 9, 4, 9, 5, 2, 4, 11, 6, 2, 10, 8, 6, 7, 9, 8, 1];

function icosphere(r, subdiv) {
  const nv = 10 * 4 ** subdiv + 2, nt = 20 * 4 ** subdiv;
  const P = new Float64Array(nv * 3);              // build in doubles, normalize at the end
  P.set(ICO_VERTS);
  let count = 12, idx = Uint32Array.from(ICO_FACES);
  for (let s = 0; s < subdiv; s++) {
    const cache = new Map(), out = new Uint32Array(idx.length * 4);
    const mid = (a, b) => {
      const key = a < b ? a * nv + b : b * nv + a;   // integer pair -> one number, no strings
      let m = cache.get(key);
      if (m === undefined) {
        m = count++;
        P[m * 3] = (P[a * 3] + P[b * 3]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 1] = (P[a * 3 + 1] + P[b * 3 + 1]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 2] = (P[a * 3 + 2] + P[b * 3 + 2]) / 2;
        // project now so later midpoints are taken between points on the sphere
        const l = Math.hypot(P[m * 3], P[m * 3 + 1], P[m * 3 + 2]) / Math.hypot(P[a * 3], P[a * 3 + 1], P[a * 3 + 2]);
        P[m * 3] /= l; P[m * 3 + 1] /= l; P[m * 3 + 2] /= l;
        cache.set(key, m);
      }
      return m;
    };
    for (let t = 0, k = 0; t < idx.length; t += 3) {
      const a = idx[t], b = idx[t + 1], c = idx[t + 2];
      const ab = mid(a, b), bc = mid(b, c), ca = mid(c, a);
      out[k++] = a; out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = ca;
      out[k++] = b; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ab;
      out[k++] = c; out[k++] = ca; out[k++] = bc;
      out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ca;
    }
    idx = out;
  }
  const m = { positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3), normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3), uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2), indices: idx };
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2], l = Math.hypot(x, y, z);
    const nx = x / l, ny = y / l, nz = z / l;
    m.normals[v * 3] = nx; m.normals[v * 3 + 1] = ny; m.normals[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
    m.positions[v * 3] = r * nx; m.positions[v * 3 + 1] = r * ny; m.positions[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
    m.uvs[v * 2] = 0.5 + Math.atan2(nz, nx) / (2 * Math.PI); m.uvs[v * 2 + 1] = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ny))) / Math.PI;
  }
  return m;
}
--- hint
`mid(a, b)`: look up the key; if missing, the new index is `count++`, its position the average of a and b scaled back to the sphere's radius, and you store it in the map.
--- hint
Normalizing midpoints at every level (not just at the end) keeps the triangles closer to equal size.
--- harness
{
  const s = icosphere(2, 2);
  eq(s.positions.length, 162 * 3, '10 * 4^2 + 2 vertices');
  eq(s.indices.length, 320 * 3, '20 * 4^2 triangles');
  eq(icosphere(1, 0).indices.length, 60, 'subdiv 0 is the icosahedron');
  const P = s.positions, I = s.indices, N = s.normals;
  let onSphere = true, unit = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i += 3) {
    if (Math.abs(Math.hypot(P[i], P[i + 1], P[i + 2]) - 2) > 1e-5) onSphere = false;
    if (Math.abs(N[i] * 2 - P[i]) > 1e-5 || Math.abs(N[i + 2] * 2 - P[i + 2]) > 1e-5) unit = false;
  }
  check(onSphere, 'every vertex on the sphere of radius 2');
  check(unit, 'normal = position / r');
  const dir = new Set();
  let out = true;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) dir.add(I[t + k] * 1000 + I[t + (k + 1) % 3]);
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2], vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    if ((uy * vz - uz * vy) * P[a] + (uz * vx - ux * vz) * P[a + 1] + (ux * vy - uy * vx) * P[a + 2] <= 0) out = false;
  }
  check(out, 'outward faces');
  check(dir.size === I.length && [...dir].every(k => dir.has((k % 1000) * 1000 + Math.floor(k / 1000))), 'closed: every directed edge once, and its reverse');
  let ok = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.uvs.length; i++) if (!(s.uvs[i] >= 0 && s.uvs[i] <= 1)) ok = false;
  check(ok, 'uvs in 0..1');
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const big = icosphere(1, 7);
  const ms = performance.now() - t0;
  eq(big.positions.length, 163842 * 3, 'subdiv 7 vertex count: midpoints shared');
  check(ms < 800, `subdiv 7 took ${ms.toFixed(0)} ms (limit 800)`);
}
=== exercise mesh-displace
title: 6. Noise displacement
continues: mesh-icosphere
--- prompt
Write `displace(mesh, noise, amp, freq, octaves = 5)`: move every vertex along its current normal by `amp * fbm3(noise, x*freq, y*freq, z*freq, octaves)` (x, y, z = its position before moving), in place, then recompute the normals with `computeNormals`, and return the mesh.

Sample noise once per vertex at its 3D position, so the mesh stays closed (the uvSphere's duplicated seam vertices move identically) and there is no seam or pole artifact. Don't allocate new arrays: the test checks the same `positions` array comes back.
--- stubs
function displace(mesh, noise, amp, freq, octaves = 5) {
  return mesh;
}
--- solution
// ---- From earlier chapters: seeded PRNG and 3D Perlin noise + fBm (p2-noise) ----
function mulberry32(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
// Improved Perlin noise (Perlin 2002) with a seeded permutation table. Returns roughly [-1, 1].
function makePerlin3(seed) {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed), p = new Uint8Array(512), perm = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) perm[i] = i;
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--) { const j = (rng() * (i + 1)) | 0, t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) p[i] = perm[i & 255];
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + t * (b - a);
  const grad = (h, x, y, z) => {
    h &= 15;
    const u = h < 8 ? x : y, v = h < 4 ? y : (h === 12 || h === 14 ? x : z);
    return ((h & 1) ? -u : u) + ((h & 2) ? -v : v);
  };
  return function (x, y, z) {
    const X = Math.floor(x), Y = Math.floor(y), Z = Math.floor(z);
    x -= X; y -= Y; z -= Z;
    const xi = X & 255, yi = Y & 255, zi = Z & 255;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y), w = fade(z);
    const A = p[xi] + yi, AA = p[A] + zi, AB = p[A + 1] + zi, B = p[xi + 1] + yi, BA = p[B] + zi, BB = p[B + 1] + zi;
    return lerp(
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA], x, y, z), grad(p[BA], x - 1, y, z), u), lerp(grad(p[AB], x, y - 1, z), grad(p[BB], x - 1, y - 1, z), u), v),
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA + 1], x, y, z - 1), grad(p[BA + 1], x - 1, y, z - 1), u), lerp(grad(p[AB + 1], x, y - 1, z - 1), grad(p[BB + 1], x - 1, y - 1, z - 1), u), v),
      w);
  };
}
function fbm3(noise, x, y, z, octaves = 5, lacunarity = 2, gain = 0.5) {
  let sum = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1;
  for (let o = 0; o < octaves; o++) {
    sum += amp * noise(x * freq, y * freq, z * freq);
    freq *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
  }
  return sum;
}

// Indexed triangle mesh: every array allocated once at its final size.
function createMesh(nv, nt) {
  return {
    positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2),
    indices: new Uint32Array(nt * 3),
  };
}

function gridMesh(w, d, nx, nz) {
  const m = createMesh((nx + 1) * (nz + 1), nx * nz * 2);
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= nx; i++, v++) {
      P[v * 3] = -w / 2 + w * i / nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = 0; P[v * 3 + 2] = -d / 2 + d * j / nz;
      N[v * 3 + 1] = 1;
      U[v * 2] = i / nx; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / nz;
    }
  }
  for (let j = 0, k = 0; j < nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      const a = j * (nx + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + nx + 1, e = c + 1;
      I[k++] = a; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = b;
      I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = e;
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// Smooth normals: sum the unnormalized face normals (length = 2 x area) into each corner, then normalize.
function computeNormals(mesh) {
  const P = mesh.positions, I = mesh.indices, N = mesh.normals;
  N.fill(0);
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    N[a] += nx; N[a + 1] += ny; N[a + 2] += nz;
    N[b] += nx; N[b + 1] += ny; N[b + 2] += nz;
    N[c] += nx; N[c + 1] += ny; N[c + 2] += nz;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N.length; i += 3) {
    const l = Math.hypot(N[i], N[i + 1], N[i + 2]);
    if (l > 0) { N[i] /= l; N[i + 1] /= l; N[i + 2] /= l; }
  }
  return mesh;
}

// Flat shading: every triangle gets its own three vertices carrying the face normal.
function flatShade(mesh) {
  const I = mesh.indices, P = mesh.positions, U = mesh.uvs, nt = I.length / 3;
  const m = createMesh(nt * 3, nt);
  const P2 = m.positions, N2 = m.normals, U2 = m.uvs;
  for (let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const s = I[t * 3 + k], d = t * 3 + k;
      P2[d * 3] = P[s * 3]; P2[d * 3 + 1] = P[s * 3 + 1]; P2[d * 3 + 2] = P[s * 3 + 2];
      U2[d * 2] = U[s * 2]; U2[d * 2 + 1] = U[s * 2 + 1];
      m.indices[d] = d;
    }
    const a = t * 9;
    const ux = P2[a + 3] - P2[a], uy = P2[a + 4] - P2[a + 1], uz = P2[a + 5] - P2[a + 2];
    const vx = P2[a + 6] - P2[a], vy = P2[a + 7] - P2[a + 1], vz = P2[a + 8] - P2[a + 2];
    let nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1;
    nx /= l; ny /= l; nz /= l;
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k += 3) { N2[a + k] = nx; N2[a + k + 1] = ny; N2[a + k + 2] = nz; }
  }
  return m;
}

// Column-major 4x4 matrices (the layout WebGPU uses), depth range 0..1.
function perspective(fovy, aspect, near, far) {
  const f = 1 / Math.tan(fovy / 2), m = new Float32Array(16);
  m[0] = f / aspect; m[5] = f; m[10] = far / (near - far); m[11] = -1; m[14] = near * far / (near - far);
  return m;
}
function lookAt(eye, target, up) {
  let zx = eye[0] - target[0], zy = eye[1] - target[1], zz = eye[2] - target[2];
  let l = Math.hypot(zx, zy, zz); zx /= l; zy /= l; zz /= l;
  let xx = up[1] * zz - up[2] * zy, xy = up[2] * zx - up[0] * zz, xz = up[0] * zy - up[1] * zx;
  l = Math.hypot(xx, xy, xz); xx /= l; xy /= l; xz /= l;
  const yx = zy * xz - zz * xy, yy = zz * xx - zx * xz, yz = zx * xy - zy * xx;
  return new Float32Array([xx, yx, zx, 0, xy, yy, zy, 0, xz, yz, zz, 0,
    -(xx * eye[0] + xy * eye[1] + xz * eye[2]), -(yx * eye[0] + yy * eye[1] + yz * eye[2]), -(zx * eye[0] + zy * eye[1] + zz * eye[2]), 1]);
}
function mul4(a, b) {   // a * b
  const m = new Float32Array(16);
  for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++)
    m[c * 4 + r] = a[r] * b[c * 4] + a[4 + r] * b[c * 4 + 1] + a[8 + r] * b[c * 4 + 2] + a[12 + r] * b[c * 4 + 3];
  return m;
}
// A render target: RGBA8 color plus a float depth buffer.
function createTarget(width, height) {
  return { width, height, color: new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4), depth: new Float32Array(width * height) };
}
function clearTarget(t, r, g, b) {
  const c = t.color;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i += 4) { c[i] = r; c[i + 1] = g; c[i + 2] = b; c[i + 3] = 255; }
  t.depth.fill(Infinity);
}
function blit(ctx, t) {
  ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(t.color, t.width, t.height), 0, 0);
}

// Z-buffered, back-face-culled, Gouraud-shaded (per-vertex Lambert) rasterizer.
let scratch = new Float32Array(0);
function renderMesh(target, mesh, viewProj, opts = {}) {
  const color = opts.color ?? [200, 200, 200], ambient = opts.ambient ?? 0.2;
  let [lx, ly, lz] = opts.light ?? [0.4, 0.8, 0.45];
  const ll = Math.hypot(lx, ly, lz); lx /= ll; ly /= ll; lz /= ll;
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals, I = mesh.indices, nv = P.length / 3;
  const W = target.width, H = target.height, C = target.color, Z = target.depth, m = viewProj;
  if (scratch.length < nv * 4) scratch = new Float32Array(nv * 4);
  const S = scratch;   // per vertex: screen x, screen y, depth, intensity (w <= 0 marks it unusable)
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2];
    const cx = m[0] * x + m[4] * y + m[8] * z + m[12], cy = m[1] * x + m[5] * y + m[9] * z + m[13];
    const cz = m[2] * x + m[6] * y + m[10] * z + m[14], cw = m[3] * x + m[7] * y + m[11] * z + m[15];
    const d = N[v * 3] * lx + N[v * 3 + 1] * ly + N[v * 3 + 2] * lz;
    S[v * 4 + 3] = ambient + (1 - ambient) * (d > 0 ? d : 0);
    if (cw <= 1e-6) { S[v * 4 + 2] = NaN; continue; }
    S[v * 4] = (cx / cw * 0.5 + 0.5) * W;
    S[v * 4 + 1] = (0.5 - cy / cw * 0.5) * H;
    S[v * 4 + 2] = cz / cw;
  }
  const [cr, cg, cb] = color;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 4, b = I[t + 1] * 4, c = I[t + 2] * 4;
    const x0 = S[a], y0 = S[a + 1], z0 = S[a + 2], x1 = S[b], y1 = S[b + 1], z1 = S[b + 2], x2 = S[c], y2 = S[c + 1], z2 = S[c + 2];
    if (z0 !== z0 || z1 !== z1 || z2 !== z2) continue;              // behind the camera
    const area = (x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (x2 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
    if (area >= 0) continue;                                          // back face (y is down)
    let minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(x0, x1, x2))), maxX = Math.min(W - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(x0, x1, x2)));
    let minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(y0, y1, y2))), maxY = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(y0, y1, y2)));
    const i0 = S[a + 3], i1 = S[b + 3], i2 = S[c + 3], inv = 1 / area;
    for (let py = minY; py <= maxY; py++) {
      const sy = py + 0.5;
      for (let px = minX; px <= maxX; px++) {
        const sx = px + 0.5;
        const w0 = ((x2 - x1) * (sy - y1) - (y2 - y1) * (sx - x1)) * inv;
        const w1 = ((x0 - x2) * (sy - y2) - (y0 - y2) * (sx - x2)) * inv;
        const w2 = 1 - w0 - w1;
        if (w0 < 0 || w1 < 0 || w2 < 0) continue;
        const z = w0 * z0 + w1 * z1 + w2 * z2, p = py * W + px;
        if (z >= Z[p] || z < 0 || z > 1) continue;
        Z[p] = z;
        const k = w0 * i0 + w1 * i1 + w2 * i2, o = p * 4;
        C[o] = cr * k; C[o + 1] = cg * k; C[o + 2] = cb * k; C[o + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
  }
}

function uvSphere(r, seg, rings) {
  const m = createMesh((rings + 1) * (seg + 1), 2 * seg * (rings - 1));
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= rings; j++) {
    const th = Math.PI * j / rings, st = Math.sin(th), ct = Math.cos(th);
    for (let i = 0; i <= seg; i++, v++) {
      const ph = 2 * Math.PI * i / seg, nx = st * Math.cos(ph), nz = st * Math.sin(ph);
      N[v * 3] = nx; N[v * 3 + 1] = ct; N[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
      P[v * 3] = r * nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = r * ct; P[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
      U[v * 2] = i / seg; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / rings;
    }
  }
  let k = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < rings; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {
      const a = j * (seg + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + seg + 1, d = c + 1;
      if (j !== 0) { I[k++] = a; I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; }
      if (j !== rings - 1) { I[k++] = b; I[k++] = d; I[k++] = c; }
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// The 12 vertices (unnormalized) and 20 outward-facing (counterclockwise) faces of an icosahedron.
const ICO_T = (1 + Math.sqrt(5)) / 2;
const ICO_VERTS = [-1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T,
  0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, ICO_T, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1];
const ICO_FACES = [0, 11, 5, 0, 5, 1, 0, 1, 7, 0, 7, 10, 0, 10, 11, 1, 5, 9, 5, 11, 4, 11, 10, 2, 10, 7, 6, 7, 1, 8,
  3, 9, 4, 3, 4, 2, 3, 2, 6, 3, 6, 8, 3, 8, 9, 4, 9, 5, 2, 4, 11, 6, 2, 10, 8, 6, 7, 9, 8, 1];

function icosphere(r, subdiv) {
  const nv = 10 * 4 ** subdiv + 2, nt = 20 * 4 ** subdiv;
  const P = new Float64Array(nv * 3);              // build in doubles, normalize at the end
  P.set(ICO_VERTS);
  let count = 12, idx = Uint32Array.from(ICO_FACES);
  for (let s = 0; s < subdiv; s++) {
    const cache = new Map(), out = new Uint32Array(idx.length * 4);
    const mid = (a, b) => {
      const key = a < b ? a * nv + b : b * nv + a;   // integer pair -> one number, no strings
      let m = cache.get(key);
      if (m === undefined) {
        m = count++;
        P[m * 3] = (P[a * 3] + P[b * 3]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 1] = (P[a * 3 + 1] + P[b * 3 + 1]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 2] = (P[a * 3 + 2] + P[b * 3 + 2]) / 2;
        // project now so later midpoints are taken between points on the sphere
        const l = Math.hypot(P[m * 3], P[m * 3 + 1], P[m * 3 + 2]) / Math.hypot(P[a * 3], P[a * 3 + 1], P[a * 3 + 2]);
        P[m * 3] /= l; P[m * 3 + 1] /= l; P[m * 3 + 2] /= l;
        cache.set(key, m);
      }
      return m;
    };
    for (let t = 0, k = 0; t < idx.length; t += 3) {
      const a = idx[t], b = idx[t + 1], c = idx[t + 2];
      const ab = mid(a, b), bc = mid(b, c), ca = mid(c, a);
      out[k++] = a; out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = ca;
      out[k++] = b; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ab;
      out[k++] = c; out[k++] = ca; out[k++] = bc;
      out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ca;
    }
    idx = out;
  }
  const m = { positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3), normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3), uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2), indices: idx };
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2], l = Math.hypot(x, y, z);
    const nx = x / l, ny = y / l, nz = z / l;
    m.normals[v * 3] = nx; m.normals[v * 3 + 1] = ny; m.normals[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
    m.positions[v * 3] = r * nx; m.positions[v * 3 + 1] = r * ny; m.positions[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
    m.uvs[v * 2] = 0.5 + Math.atan2(nz, nx) / (2 * Math.PI); m.uvs[v * 2 + 1] = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ny))) / Math.PI;
  }
  return m;
}

// Move every vertex along its normal by amp * fBm(position * freq), then rebuild the normals.
function displace(mesh, noise, amp, freq, octaves = 5) {
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals;
  for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i += 3) {
    const h = amp * fbm3(noise, P[i] * freq, P[i + 1] * freq, P[i + 2] * freq, octaves);
    P[i] += N[i] * h; P[i + 1] += N[i + 1] * h; P[i + 2] += N[i + 2] * h;
  }
  return computeNormals(mesh);
}
--- hint
Look at a planet: `const t = createTarget(canvas.width, canvas.height); clearTarget(t, 10, 12, 20); renderMesh(t, displace(icosphere(1, 5), makePerlin3(1), 0.15, 2), mul4(perspective(0.9, 1, 0.1, 100), lookAt([0, 1, 3.2], [0, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0])), { color: [120, 180, 110] }); blit(canvas.getContext('2d'), t);`
--- harness
{
  const noise = makePerlin3(5), ref = icosphere(1, 3), m = icosphere(1, 3), P0 = m.positions;
  const r = displace(m, noise, 0.2, 2, 4);
  check(r === m && r.positions === P0, 'in place: same mesh, same positions array');
  let maxErr = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < ref.positions.length; i += 3) {
    const h = 0.2 * fbm3(noise, ref.positions[i] * 2, ref.positions[i + 1] * 2, ref.positions[i + 2] * 2, 4);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) maxErr = Math.max(maxErr, Math.abs(ref.positions[i + k] + ref.normals[i + k] * h - m.positions[i + k]));
  }
  check(maxErr < 1e-5, 'each vertex moved by amp * fbm along its normal');
  const copy = { positions: m.positions.slice(), normals: new Float32Array(m.normals.length), uvs: m.uvs, indices: m.indices };
  computeNormals(copy);
  let nErr = 0; for (let i = 0; i < copy.normals.length; i++) nErr = Math.max(nErr, Math.abs(copy.normals[i] - m.normals[i]));
  check(nErr < 1e-5, 'normals recomputed after moving');
  const u = displace(uvSphere(1, 24, 12), noise, 0.3, 1.5);
  let seam = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j <= 12; j++) for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) seam = Math.max(seam, Math.abs(u.positions[(j * 25) * 3 + k] - u.positions[(j * 25 + 24) * 3 + k]));
  check(seam < 1e-6, 'uv sphere seam stays closed');
  let moved = 0; for (let i = 0; i < P0.length; i++) if (Math.abs(P0[i] - ref.positions[i]) > 1e-3) moved++;
  check(moved > P0.length / 2, 'most vertices moved');
}
=== exercise mesh-tube
title: 7. Tubes with parallel transport
continues: mesh-displace
--- prompt
Write `tubeMesh(path, radii, sides)`: `path` is a `Float32Array` of n points (x, y, z); `radii` is a number or a `Float32Array` of n radii. Output: `n * (sides + 1)` vertices (a seam column), `2 * sides * (n − 1)` triangles, open ends.

Frames, in this exact way so results match:

- Tangent `T_i` = normalized `p[i+1] − p[i−1]`, with the indices clamped to the ends (so the first is `p1 − p0`).
- First normal: if `|T_0.x| < 0.9` start from (1, 0, 0), else (0, 1, 0). For **every** i (including 0): `N = normalize(N − T_i (T_i · N))`, using the previous N. Then `B = T_i × N`.
- Ring vertex `k = 0..sides` at angle `a = 2πk / sides`: direction `o = cos a · N + sin a · B`, position `p_i + r_i · o`, normal `o`, uv `(k/sides, i/(n−1))`.
- Triangles between ring i and i+1, with `a = i*(sides+1)+k`, `b = a+1`, `c = a+sides+1`, `d = c+1`: `(a, b, c)`, `(b, d, c)`.

**Avoid** Frenet frames (normal along the curvature): they are undefined on straight pieces and flip at inflections. The test sweeps an S-curve and checks B never flips.
--- stubs
function tubeMesh(path, radii, sides) {
  return createMesh(0, 0);
}
--- solution
// ---- From earlier chapters: seeded PRNG and 3D Perlin noise + fBm (p2-noise) ----
function mulberry32(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
// Improved Perlin noise (Perlin 2002) with a seeded permutation table. Returns roughly [-1, 1].
function makePerlin3(seed) {
  const rng = mulberry32(seed), p = new Uint8Array(512), perm = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) perm[i] = i;
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--) { const j = (rng() * (i + 1)) | 0, t = perm[i]; perm[i] = perm[j]; perm[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) p[i] = perm[i & 255];
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + t * (b - a);
  const grad = (h, x, y, z) => {
    h &= 15;
    const u = h < 8 ? x : y, v = h < 4 ? y : (h === 12 || h === 14 ? x : z);
    return ((h & 1) ? -u : u) + ((h & 2) ? -v : v);
  };
  return function (x, y, z) {
    const X = Math.floor(x), Y = Math.floor(y), Z = Math.floor(z);
    x -= X; y -= Y; z -= Z;
    const xi = X & 255, yi = Y & 255, zi = Z & 255;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y), w = fade(z);
    const A = p[xi] + yi, AA = p[A] + zi, AB = p[A + 1] + zi, B = p[xi + 1] + yi, BA = p[B] + zi, BB = p[B + 1] + zi;
    return lerp(
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA], x, y, z), grad(p[BA], x - 1, y, z), u), lerp(grad(p[AB], x, y - 1, z), grad(p[BB], x - 1, y - 1, z), u), v),
      lerp(lerp(grad(p[AA + 1], x, y, z - 1), grad(p[BA + 1], x - 1, y, z - 1), u), lerp(grad(p[AB + 1], x, y - 1, z - 1), grad(p[BB + 1], x - 1, y - 1, z - 1), u), v),
      w);
  };
}
function fbm3(noise, x, y, z, octaves = 5, lacunarity = 2, gain = 0.5) {
  let sum = 0, amp = 1, freq = 1;
  for (let o = 0; o < octaves; o++) {
    sum += amp * noise(x * freq, y * freq, z * freq);
    freq *= lacunarity; amp *= gain;
  }
  return sum;
}

// Indexed triangle mesh: every array allocated once at its final size.
function createMesh(nv, nt) {
  return {
    positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3),
    uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2),
    indices: new Uint32Array(nt * 3),
  };
}

function gridMesh(w, d, nx, nz) {
  const m = createMesh((nx + 1) * (nz + 1), nx * nz * 2);
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i <= nx; i++, v++) {
      P[v * 3] = -w / 2 + w * i / nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = 0; P[v * 3 + 2] = -d / 2 + d * j / nz;
      N[v * 3 + 1] = 1;
      U[v * 2] = i / nx; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / nz;
    }
  }
  for (let j = 0, k = 0; j < nz; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      const a = j * (nx + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + nx + 1, e = c + 1;
      I[k++] = a; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = b;
      I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; I[k++] = e;
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// Smooth normals: sum the unnormalized face normals (length = 2 x area) into each corner, then normalize.
function computeNormals(mesh) {
  const P = mesh.positions, I = mesh.indices, N = mesh.normals;
  N.fill(0);
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 3, b = I[t + 1] * 3, c = I[t + 2] * 3;
    const ux = P[b] - P[a], uy = P[b + 1] - P[a + 1], uz = P[b + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const vx = P[c] - P[a], vy = P[c + 1] - P[a + 1], vz = P[c + 2] - P[a + 2];
    const nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    N[a] += nx; N[a + 1] += ny; N[a + 2] += nz;
    N[b] += nx; N[b + 1] += ny; N[b + 2] += nz;
    N[c] += nx; N[c + 1] += ny; N[c + 2] += nz;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < N.length; i += 3) {
    const l = Math.hypot(N[i], N[i + 1], N[i + 2]);
    if (l > 0) { N[i] /= l; N[i + 1] /= l; N[i + 2] /= l; }
  }
  return mesh;
}

// Flat shading: every triangle gets its own three vertices carrying the face normal.
function flatShade(mesh) {
  const I = mesh.indices, P = mesh.positions, U = mesh.uvs, nt = I.length / 3;
  const m = createMesh(nt * 3, nt);
  const P2 = m.positions, N2 = m.normals, U2 = m.uvs;
  for (let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      const s = I[t * 3 + k], d = t * 3 + k;
      P2[d * 3] = P[s * 3]; P2[d * 3 + 1] = P[s * 3 + 1]; P2[d * 3 + 2] = P[s * 3 + 2];
      U2[d * 2] = U[s * 2]; U2[d * 2 + 1] = U[s * 2 + 1];
      m.indices[d] = d;
    }
    const a = t * 9;
    const ux = P2[a + 3] - P2[a], uy = P2[a + 4] - P2[a + 1], uz = P2[a + 5] - P2[a + 2];
    const vx = P2[a + 6] - P2[a], vy = P2[a + 7] - P2[a + 1], vz = P2[a + 8] - P2[a + 2];
    let nx = uy * vz - uz * vy, ny = uz * vx - ux * vz, nz = ux * vy - uy * vx;
    const l = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz) || 1;
    nx /= l; ny /= l; nz /= l;
    for (let k = 0; k < 9; k += 3) { N2[a + k] = nx; N2[a + k + 1] = ny; N2[a + k + 2] = nz; }
  }
  return m;
}

// Column-major 4x4 matrices (the layout WebGPU uses), depth range 0..1.
function perspective(fovy, aspect, near, far) {
  const f = 1 / Math.tan(fovy / 2), m = new Float32Array(16);
  m[0] = f / aspect; m[5] = f; m[10] = far / (near - far); m[11] = -1; m[14] = near * far / (near - far);
  return m;
}
function lookAt(eye, target, up) {
  let zx = eye[0] - target[0], zy = eye[1] - target[1], zz = eye[2] - target[2];
  let l = Math.hypot(zx, zy, zz); zx /= l; zy /= l; zz /= l;
  let xx = up[1] * zz - up[2] * zy, xy = up[2] * zx - up[0] * zz, xz = up[0] * zy - up[1] * zx;
  l = Math.hypot(xx, xy, xz); xx /= l; xy /= l; xz /= l;
  const yx = zy * xz - zz * xy, yy = zz * xx - zx * xz, yz = zx * xy - zy * xx;
  return new Float32Array([xx, yx, zx, 0, xy, yy, zy, 0, xz, yz, zz, 0,
    -(xx * eye[0] + xy * eye[1] + xz * eye[2]), -(yx * eye[0] + yy * eye[1] + yz * eye[2]), -(zx * eye[0] + zy * eye[1] + zz * eye[2]), 1]);
}
function mul4(a, b) {   // a * b
  const m = new Float32Array(16);
  for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++)
    m[c * 4 + r] = a[r] * b[c * 4] + a[4 + r] * b[c * 4 + 1] + a[8 + r] * b[c * 4 + 2] + a[12 + r] * b[c * 4 + 3];
  return m;
}
// A render target: RGBA8 color plus a float depth buffer.
function createTarget(width, height) {
  return { width, height, color: new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4), depth: new Float32Array(width * height) };
}
function clearTarget(t, r, g, b) {
  const c = t.color;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i += 4) { c[i] = r; c[i + 1] = g; c[i + 2] = b; c[i + 3] = 255; }
  t.depth.fill(Infinity);
}
function blit(ctx, t) {
  ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(t.color, t.width, t.height), 0, 0);
}

// Z-buffered, back-face-culled, Gouraud-shaded (per-vertex Lambert) rasterizer.
let scratch = new Float32Array(0);
function renderMesh(target, mesh, viewProj, opts = {}) {
  const color = opts.color ?? [200, 200, 200], ambient = opts.ambient ?? 0.2;
  let [lx, ly, lz] = opts.light ?? [0.4, 0.8, 0.45];
  const ll = Math.hypot(lx, ly, lz); lx /= ll; ly /= ll; lz /= ll;
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals, I = mesh.indices, nv = P.length / 3;
  const W = target.width, H = target.height, C = target.color, Z = target.depth, m = viewProj;
  if (scratch.length < nv * 4) scratch = new Float32Array(nv * 4);
  const S = scratch;   // per vertex: screen x, screen y, depth, intensity (w <= 0 marks it unusable)
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2];
    const cx = m[0] * x + m[4] * y + m[8] * z + m[12], cy = m[1] * x + m[5] * y + m[9] * z + m[13];
    const cz = m[2] * x + m[6] * y + m[10] * z + m[14], cw = m[3] * x + m[7] * y + m[11] * z + m[15];
    const d = N[v * 3] * lx + N[v * 3 + 1] * ly + N[v * 3 + 2] * lz;
    S[v * 4 + 3] = ambient + (1 - ambient) * (d > 0 ? d : 0);
    if (cw <= 1e-6) { S[v * 4 + 2] = NaN; continue; }
    S[v * 4] = (cx / cw * 0.5 + 0.5) * W;
    S[v * 4 + 1] = (0.5 - cy / cw * 0.5) * H;
    S[v * 4 + 2] = cz / cw;
  }
  const [cr, cg, cb] = color;
  for (let t = 0; t < I.length; t += 3) {
    const a = I[t] * 4, b = I[t + 1] * 4, c = I[t + 2] * 4;
    const x0 = S[a], y0 = S[a + 1], z0 = S[a + 2], x1 = S[b], y1 = S[b + 1], z1 = S[b + 2], x2 = S[c], y2 = S[c + 1], z2 = S[c + 2];
    if (z0 !== z0 || z1 !== z1 || z2 !== z2) continue;              // behind the camera
    const area = (x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (x2 - x0) * (y1 - y0);
    if (area >= 0) continue;                                          // back face (y is down)
    let minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(x0, x1, x2))), maxX = Math.min(W - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(x0, x1, x2)));
    let minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.min(y0, y1, y2))), maxY = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(Math.max(y0, y1, y2)));
    const i0 = S[a + 3], i1 = S[b + 3], i2 = S[c + 3], inv = 1 / area;
    for (let py = minY; py <= maxY; py++) {
      const sy = py + 0.5;
      for (let px = minX; px <= maxX; px++) {
        const sx = px + 0.5;
        const w0 = ((x2 - x1) * (sy - y1) - (y2 - y1) * (sx - x1)) * inv;
        const w1 = ((x0 - x2) * (sy - y2) - (y0 - y2) * (sx - x2)) * inv;
        const w2 = 1 - w0 - w1;
        if (w0 < 0 || w1 < 0 || w2 < 0) continue;
        const z = w0 * z0 + w1 * z1 + w2 * z2, p = py * W + px;
        if (z >= Z[p] || z < 0 || z > 1) continue;
        Z[p] = z;
        const k = w0 * i0 + w1 * i1 + w2 * i2, o = p * 4;
        C[o] = cr * k; C[o + 1] = cg * k; C[o + 2] = cb * k; C[o + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
  }
}

function uvSphere(r, seg, rings) {
  const m = createMesh((rings + 1) * (seg + 1), 2 * seg * (rings - 1));
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  for (let j = 0, v = 0; j <= rings; j++) {
    const th = Math.PI * j / rings, st = Math.sin(th), ct = Math.cos(th);
    for (let i = 0; i <= seg; i++, v++) {
      const ph = 2 * Math.PI * i / seg, nx = st * Math.cos(ph), nz = st * Math.sin(ph);
      N[v * 3] = nx; N[v * 3 + 1] = ct; N[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
      P[v * 3] = r * nx; P[v * 3 + 1] = r * ct; P[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
      U[v * 2] = i / seg; U[v * 2 + 1] = j / rings;
    }
  }
  let k = 0;
  for (let j = 0; j < rings; j++) {
    for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {
      const a = j * (seg + 1) + i, b = a + 1, c = a + seg + 1, d = c + 1;
      if (j !== 0) { I[k++] = a; I[k++] = b; I[k++] = c; }
      if (j !== rings - 1) { I[k++] = b; I[k++] = d; I[k++] = c; }
    }
  }
  return m;
}

// The 12 vertices (unnormalized) and 20 outward-facing (counterclockwise) faces of an icosahedron.
const ICO_T = (1 + Math.sqrt(5)) / 2;
const ICO_VERTS = [-1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, ICO_T,
  0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, ICO_T, 0, -1, ICO_T, 0, 1, -ICO_T, 0, -1, -ICO_T, 0, 1];
const ICO_FACES = [0, 11, 5, 0, 5, 1, 0, 1, 7, 0, 7, 10, 0, 10, 11, 1, 5, 9, 5, 11, 4, 11, 10, 2, 10, 7, 6, 7, 1, 8,
  3, 9, 4, 3, 4, 2, 3, 2, 6, 3, 6, 8, 3, 8, 9, 4, 9, 5, 2, 4, 11, 6, 2, 10, 8, 6, 7, 9, 8, 1];

function icosphere(r, subdiv) {
  const nv = 10 * 4 ** subdiv + 2, nt = 20 * 4 ** subdiv;
  const P = new Float64Array(nv * 3);              // build in doubles, normalize at the end
  P.set(ICO_VERTS);
  let count = 12, idx = Uint32Array.from(ICO_FACES);
  for (let s = 0; s < subdiv; s++) {
    const cache = new Map(), out = new Uint32Array(idx.length * 4);
    const mid = (a, b) => {
      const key = a < b ? a * nv + b : b * nv + a;   // integer pair -> one number, no strings
      let m = cache.get(key);
      if (m === undefined) {
        m = count++;
        P[m * 3] = (P[a * 3] + P[b * 3]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 1] = (P[a * 3 + 1] + P[b * 3 + 1]) / 2; P[m * 3 + 2] = (P[a * 3 + 2] + P[b * 3 + 2]) / 2;
        // project now so later midpoints are taken between points on the sphere
        const l = Math.hypot(P[m * 3], P[m * 3 + 1], P[m * 3 + 2]) / Math.hypot(P[a * 3], P[a * 3 + 1], P[a * 3 + 2]);
        P[m * 3] /= l; P[m * 3 + 1] /= l; P[m * 3 + 2] /= l;
        cache.set(key, m);
      }
      return m;
    };
    for (let t = 0, k = 0; t < idx.length; t += 3) {
      const a = idx[t], b = idx[t + 1], c = idx[t + 2];
      const ab = mid(a, b), bc = mid(b, c), ca = mid(c, a);
      out[k++] = a; out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = ca;
      out[k++] = b; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ab;
      out[k++] = c; out[k++] = ca; out[k++] = bc;
      out[k++] = ab; out[k++] = bc; out[k++] = ca;
    }
    idx = out;
  }
  const m = { positions: new Float32Array(nv * 3), normals: new Float32Array(nv * 3), uvs: new Float32Array(nv * 2), indices: idx };
  for (let v = 0; v < nv; v++) {
    const x = P[v * 3], y = P[v * 3 + 1], z = P[v * 3 + 2], l = Math.hypot(x, y, z);
    const nx = x / l, ny = y / l, nz = z / l;
    m.normals[v * 3] = nx; m.normals[v * 3 + 1] = ny; m.normals[v * 3 + 2] = nz;
    m.positions[v * 3] = r * nx; m.positions[v * 3 + 1] = r * ny; m.positions[v * 3 + 2] = r * nz;
    m.uvs[v * 2] = 0.5 + Math.atan2(nz, nx) / (2 * Math.PI); m.uvs[v * 2 + 1] = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ny))) / Math.PI;
  }
  return m;
}

// Move every vertex along its normal by amp * fBm(position * freq), then rebuild the normals.
function displace(mesh, noise, amp, freq, octaves = 5) {
  const P = mesh.positions, N = mesh.normals;
  for (let i = 0; i < P.length; i += 3) {
    const h = amp * fbm3(noise, P[i] * freq, P[i + 1] * freq, P[i + 2] * freq, octaves);
    P[i] += N[i] * h; P[i + 1] += N[i + 1] * h; P[i + 2] += N[i + 2] * h;
  }
  return computeNormals(mesh);
}

// A tube along a polyline with parallel-transport (rotation-minimizing) frames.
function tubeMesh(path, radii, sides) {
  const n = path.length / 3, ring = sides + 1;
  const m = createMesh(n * ring, 2 * sides * (n - 1));
  const { positions: P, normals: N, uvs: U, indices: I } = m;
  let nx = 0, ny = 0, nz = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = Math.max(i - 1, 0) * 3, b = Math.min(i + 1, n - 1) * 3;
    let tx = path[b] - path[a], ty = path[b + 1] - path[a + 1], tz = path[b + 2] - path[a + 2];
    const tl = Math.hypot(tx, ty, tz); tx /= tl; ty /= tl; tz /= tl;
    if (i === 0) { if (Math.abs(tx) < 0.9) { nx = 1; ny = 0; nz = 0; } else { nx = 0; ny = 1; nz = 0; } }
    // project the previous normal onto the plane perpendicular to the new tangent
    const d = nx * tx + ny * ty + nz * tz;
    nx -= tx * d; ny -= ty * d; nz -= tz * d;
    const nl = Math.hypot(nx, ny, nz); nx /= nl; ny /= nl; nz /= nl;
    const bx = ty * nz - tz * ny, by = tz * nx - tx * nz, bz = tx * ny - ty * nx;
    const r = typeof radii === 'number' ? radii : radii[i];
    for (let k = 0; k <= sides; k++) {
      const ang = 2 * Math.PI * k / sides, c = Math.cos(ang), s = Math.sin(ang), v = i * ring + k;
      const ox = c * nx + s * bx, oy = c * ny + s * by, oz = c * nz + s * bz;
      N[v * 3] = ox; N[v * 3 + 1] = oy; N[v * 3 + 2] = oz;
      P[v * 3] = path[i * 3] + r * ox; P[v * 3 + 1] = path[i * 3 + 1] + r * oy; P[v * 3 + 2] = path[i * 3 + 2] + r * oz;
      U[v * 2] = k / sides; U[v * 2 + 1] = n > 1 ? i / (n - 1) : 0;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0, t = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < sides; k++) {
      const a = i * ring + k, b = a + 1, c = a + ring, d = c + 1;
      I[t++] = a; I[t++] = b; I[t++] = c;
      I[t++] = b; I[t++] = d; I[t++] = c;
    }
  }
  return m;
}
--- hint
Carry `nx, ny, nz` across loop iterations; each iteration only projects and renormalizes it.
--- hint
Look at it: a helix `const p = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 200; i++) { const t = i / 200 * 12; p.push(Math.cos(t), t / 6 - 1, Math.sin(t)); }` then `renderMesh(t, tubeMesh(new Float32Array(p), 0.15, 16), ...)` as in earlier hints.
